## W827. Store-Level Building Material Load Calculation & Safety Advisory

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Trigger** | Customer purchases structural building materials (cement, steel rebar, lumber for framing, hollow blocks for load-bearing walls) in significant quantities |
| **Frequency** | Weekly; ~50-80 advisory requests per month per store for structural material purchases |
| **Volume** | 200 stores x 60 requests/month = ~12,000 advisory interactions/month |
| **Owner** | Department Supervisor (Lumber & Building Materials) |
| **Participants** | Department Supervisor, Sales Associate, Customer |

### Background

A unique liability and safety concern in hardware retail is customers purchasing structural materials without professional engineering advice. A customer buying 200 hollow blocks and 20 bags of cement for a boundary wall, or steel rebar for a second-floor extension, may not have consulted an engineer. While BuildRight is not responsible for structural engineering, the company has a duty of care to provide basic load and safety advisory — especially for products where misuse could cause injury or death (e.g., undersized beams, insufficient rebar, wrong cement mix for structural use). Philippine building code (National Building Code, PD 1096) requires engineering plans for structural construction. This workflow provides basic guidance, warns about code requirements, and refers customers to licensed professionals — protecting both the customer and BuildRight from liability per W185 product liability.

### Steps

| # | Activity | Role (R) | Role (A) | Duration |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Sales Associate identifies customer purchasing structural materials above threshold quantities: (a) cement > 50 bags (indicates structural pour, not patching), (b) steel rebar > 20 pieces of any size (indicates reinforcement work), (c) lumber > 50 board feet designated for framing use (not finishing), (d) hollow blocks > 200 pieces (indicates wall construction); POS system flags these transactions with advisory prompt per threshold configuration | System / Sales Associate | — | Automated at scan |
| 2 | Sales Associate asks customer about project scope: "What are you building? Is this for structural or non-structural use?" — determines whether advisory is required: (a) non-structural projects (garden wall, pavers, non-load-bearing partition, fence under 2 meters): advisory not required but general guidance offered, (b) structural projects (load-bearing wall, floor slab, column, beam, foundation, second-floor extension, retaining wall > 1 meter): full advisory per steps 3-6 | Sales Associate | — | 2 min |
| 3 | If structural project identified: Sales Associate activates load calculation reference tool on POS terminal or handheld device: (a) provides general material specifications per Philippine building code — standard concrete mix ratio 1:2:4 (cement:sand:gravel) for general structural use, 1:1.5:3 for reinforced concrete; minimum rebar size for columns (12mm minimum per standard practice, 16mm preferred); standard hollow block wall thickness (150mm for load-bearing, 100mm for non-load-bearing); minimum beam depth-to-span ratio, (b) displays safety disclaimer prominently: "These are general reference guidelines only, based on standard Philippine construction practices. For structural construction, BuildRight strongly recommends consulting a licensed civil engineer per National Building Code PD 1096. BuildRight assumes no liability for structural failures resulting from customer's construction decisions.", (c) prints or displays material specification reference sheet — includes standard mix ratios, rebar spacing guidelines, concrete curing times, and engineering referral contacts (list of 3-5 licensed engineers in store's trade area) | Sales Associate | Department Supervisor | 5 min |
| 4 | Department Supervisor reviews for high-risk purchases: (a) if customer appears to be building a multi-story structure without engineering plans — customer mentions second floor, mezzanine, or roof deck; purchasing structural steel beams, large-diameter rebar (20mm+), or high-strength cement, (b) if material selection is clearly inadequate for stated purpose — e.g., 10mm rebar for columns when 16mm minimum is standard practice; buying hollow blocks for a 3-meter high boundary wall without columns; ordering insufficient cement bags for stated slab area (calculated per W823 material calculator shows shortage), (c) Supervisor provides additional advisory: reinforces recommendation to consult engineer, may decline sale of clearly inadequate materials for stated structural purpose (rare — customer advised, not refused), (d) documents additional advisory interaction in system | Department Supervisor | Store Manager | 5-10 min for high-risk cases |
| 5 | System logs advisory interaction for liability protection: (a) customer account or transaction ID, (b) materials purchased with quantities, (c) advisory provided — reference sheet printed, safety disclaimer displayed, verbal advisory confirmed, (d) customer acknowledgment — customer signs or confirms advisory received on POS terminal or delivery receipt, (e) log retained per W256 document retention policy — minimum 10 years for construction-related advisory records per Philippine legal requirement | System / Sales Associate | — | 2 min |
| 6 | Optional value-added service: Sales Associate offers referral to BuildRight's design consultancy service per W148 (project planning with professional engineer) or installation service per W138 (BuildRight-certified contractor for assembly/installation): "We can connect you with a licensed engineer who can design your project properly. Would you like to schedule a consultation?" — estimated 10-15% conversion to consultancy service | Sales Associate | — | 2 min |
| 7 | Monthly advisory analytics: (a) advisory interaction rate — percentage of structural material transactions above threshold that triggered advisory (target: 100%), (b) conversion to design/installation services — customers who used W148 or W138 after receiving advisory, (c) customer feedback on advisory helpfulness — post-interaction survey per W708, (d) advisory log completeness — percentage of advisory interactions with all required fields documented (target: >95%), (e) high-risk case frequency and outcome tracking | Department Supervisor | Store Manager | 30 min/month |

### System Touchpoints

- W5B in-store selling — advisory triggered during POS transaction for qualifying material quantities
- W185 product liability — liability framework for customer advisory and duty of care
- W138 installation services — referral to BuildRight-certified contractor for structural installation
- W148 design consultancy — referral to licensed engineer for structural design
- W252 item master — material specifications, standard dimensions, and load ratings
- W156 CDP (customer data platform) — customer project tracking and advisory history
- W255 document storage — advisory interaction logs retained for liability protection
- W41 complaint resolution — handling advisory-related customer complaints (perceived interference, advisory accuracy disputes)
- W823 material calculator — quantity estimation integrated with structural threshold detection

### Pain Points / Risks

- **Customer resistance to advisory** — some customers perceive advisory as upselling, interference, or questioning their competence; "I know what I'm doing, just sell me the materials"; Sales Associate must frame advisory as helpful service and legal compliance, not judgment; customer may take business to competitor if advisory feels burdensome
- **Sales Associate knowledge limitations** — Sales Associates are not engineers; they can relay standard reference guidelines but cannot provide project-specific structural advice; clear boundary between "general reference" and "engineering advice" must be maintained in training; advisory tool provides scripted, approved reference content
- **Liability exposure if advisory is wrong or insufficient** — if BuildRight's reference tool provides incorrect standard mix ratios or rebar sizing, and customer relies on it, BuildRight may face liability; mitigated by prominent disclaimer, referral to licensed professionals, and regular review of reference content by structural engineer consultant
- **Customer proceeding despite warnings** — customer receives advisory, understands risks, and proceeds with inadequate materials anyway; BuildRight's liability is limited by documented advisory and customer acknowledgment; advisory log is critical legal defense per W185
- **Non-English speaking customers requiring vernacular advisory materials** — advisory reference sheets must be available in English and Filipino (Tagalog); for Visayan-speaking customers (Visayas/Mindanao stores), key materials should be available in Cebuano/Bisaya; visual guides (diagrams) supplement language-specific materials
- **Threshold calibration** — thresholds set too low generate excessive advisory interactions (wasting Sales Associate time on obviously non-structural purchases); thresholds set too high miss customers who need advisory; quarterly review of threshold effectiveness per W827.7 analytics

### Time Estimate

Per advisory: 5-15 min (2 min threshold identification + 2 min project scope inquiry + 5 min reference tool and advisory + 2 min documentation + optional 2 min service referral). High-risk escalation: additional 5-10 min with Department Supervisor. Weekly per store: ~12-20 advisory interactions x 10 min avg = ~2-3 hours/week. Monthly analytics: 30 min. Chain-wide: ~400-600 hours/month for advisory interactions.

### Staffing Implication

- Absorbed by existing Department Supervisor and Sales Associates as part of selling duties in Lumber & Building Materials department
- Training on Philippine building code basics, standard material specifications, and advisory protocol per W518 — 3-hour training module for all Lumber & Building Materials department staff
- Department Supervisor certification: must complete advanced building materials training (8-hour course covering structural basics, load calculation reference, liability framework, and high-risk case identification)
- Quarterly refresher training on advisory protocol and reference tool updates — 30 min per session
- No incremental headcount — advisory is a liability protection measure integrated into standard selling process
- Engineering referral network: each store maintains a list of 3-5 licensed civil engineers in the trade area; updated semi-annually by Department Supervisor
